# North-star gap matrix

**Status:** living document, regenerated when program rounds land.
**Measured against:** main at 780e31fa (code tree surveyed 2026-09-29; main has since gained ca8b3836 and 15 earlier commits — re-verify rows those touch). Spec: [`agent-friday-ideal-product-spec.md`](agent-friday-ideal-product-spec.md) as amended by [`AMENDMENTS.md`](AMENDMENTS.md).
**Method:** 13 agents each took one block of sections, extracted every normative requirement and mapped it to the code (a file and line, a symbol or a test). For each block, a second agent then tried to refute its *shipped*, *missing* and *conflicting* rows (up to 40 per block) and corrected the ones that did not hold. The scenarios (§38) and glossary (§39) are not normative and are not mapped.

Status meanings:
- **shipped**: built and wired, with evidence.
- **partial**: some of it exists.
- **proposed**: specified in a `docs/design/active` spec but not built.
- **missing**: neither built nor specified.
- **conflicting**: the product deliberately does something incompatible; the pick is recorded below.
- **obsolete**: superseded by an amendment.

## Conflicts and the pick

The owner's rulings in AMENDMENTS.md override the spec. Where no amendment or earlier owner ruling covers a conflict, the pick is the program's own, made under the delegation recorded in `docs/decisions/2026-09-29-program-delegated-decisions.md`. Picks marked **Owner call** are reported to the owner to confirm.

| Req | Requirement | Current product | Pick | Consequence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NS-7.2-1 | The core product is not a creator marketplace. | A Marketplace workspace is in the core dock (index.html:5957); services/marketplace.py; routes/federation.py:365 /api/marketplace/purchase. | Spec, for defaults. **Owner call** | Purchases are already off (D9). The Marketplace is not in the fresh-install dock. Whether the creator economy stays in the product is the owner's decision. |
| NS-7.2-2 | The core product is not a cryptocurrency or compute-rental economy. | Positron (mψ) economy in services/economy.py with UI (index.html:16164, 16267); services/compute_provider.py accepts federated compute jobs (routes/compute.py:41 /api/federation/compute/request). | Spec, for defaults. **Owner call** | Federated compute stays off by default. Positrons stay as brand vocabulary for reputation. Whether they carry a price is the owner's call (workspace-ecosystem §6.3). |
| NS-7.3-1 | Federation, agent-to-agent collaboration, skill marketplace and distributed compute stay absent from the core UX until the local product is dependable and security-reviewed. | Marketplace is in the dock (index.html:5957); federation routes (routes/federation.py, routes/compute.py); peer and federation endpoints exposed. v6-wholeness-spec.md §1.1 'scope by subtraction' agrees they should be staged, but the code ships them. | Spec, for defaults. **Owner call** | Federation and marketplace surfaces stay out of the default dock until the local product passes §34. |

A later piece. |

| NS-10.6-2 | No self-modification merely because a weekly review found an opportunity; behaviour changes arrive as proposals. | core/__init__.py:2344-2346 learning_loop enabled:True, 'promotes the best into the system prompt'; services/learning_loop.py:373-384 run_epoch mine+promote; scheduler.py:1355-1361 weekly learning_epoch | A2 (owner ruling) | Learning-loop promotions become proposals with a diff, evidence, a preview and rollback. ## Where the spec is wrong about today's product

- (§13-15) The prior row NS-14.5-2 said no summarization exists. services/compaction.py summarizes long agent-loop transcripts and keeps a lossless durable record.
- (§13-15) off_record.receipt_view/RECEIPT_FIELDS is not used anywhere. Minimal receipts are enforced in approvals._disk_view and activity_ledger instead.
- (§18-19) Rows cite services/action_gate.py; the file is actually src/agent_friday/governance/action_gate.py (line ranges match approximately).
- (§25-26) Spec §25.7 implies there is no minor mode at all. A whole-install minor_mode setting ships: core/__init__.py:2445, set during setup at services/setup_chat.py:372, enforced in creative_engine.py:298-343, music_engine.py:227 and creative_policy.py:152. It covers only the creative and music content filters, though. It does not cover spending, time limits or attachment behaviour, so NS-25.7-6/7/9 stay missing.
- (§27-29) §27.7 implicitly treats sync as not built. In fact a paired-device settings sync ships: routes/federation.py:173 /api/federation/settings/sync, end-to-end encrypted through services/federation_transport.py. It applies peer values with silent last-write-wins (routes/federation.py:615).
- (§30-32) NS-32.17-1 prior evidence said dissent_gate covers harm, not instruction conflicts; in fact dissent_gate.check_dissent compares the action against user constraints/goals (interest_model) and attaches the conflict to approval cards, with a golden test for acting after being told not to.
- (§30-32) NS-32.2-2: Friday does propose a cloud route (cloud_spill.py approval card) when the local seat is busy; only the failure-fallback ladder substitutes automatically.
- (§33-35) §33.6/§35 assume crash-free rate via opt-in diagnostics; overridden by A3 (no telemetry even opt-in) — measure via test infra/local Doctor only.
- (§33-35) §34.2 implies security suites run independently; in fact .github/workflows/tests.yml runs security+egress as a later step in the same pytest job without if: always(), so a unit/API failure skips them.
- (§33-35) Row NS-33.1-13 said there is no minimum touch-target rule in ui_parts/head.html. head.html:1158 has .dock-btn min-height: 44px, and styles_and_scene.html:50 has .ws-tab-head min-height: 44px.
- (§33-35) Row NS-33.5-5 said there is no declared offline mode. model_routing.mode 'local_only' is described in code as the 'Airplane / Sovereign mode' (services/cloud_voice.py:414) and can be chosen in the UI (ui_parts/app.html:20433).
- (§33-35) Row NS-33.5-3 treated FRIDAY_SAFE_MODE as spec safe mode. Per boot_guard.py:26-30 it only disables self-modification, which falls short of spec §28.5.
- (§33-35) Row NS-33.4-6 listed per-scope stops only. There is also POST /api/control/kill (routes/control.py:121-135), a UI kill-switch button (ui_parts/app.html:20554, Ctrl+Shift+Q), but it only covers computer control. The row's status (missing a global kill) still stands.
- (§36-37) Phase 0 lists 'receipt viewer foundation' as future work, but an activity ledger panel with a reasoning-traces tab (index.html:55960, 37648; routes/activity.py:26) and a signed morning receipt (index.html:22475; services/morning_receipt.py; routes/goals.py:325-342) already ship.
- (§36-37) Phase 0 lists 'capability registry tied to live dependencies' as future work; services/capability_state.py with GET /api/capabilities/state (routes/core_routes.py:1106) already exists.
- (§36-37) Phase 3 lists 'exact approvals' and 'grant constraints' as future work; fingerprinted one-approval-one-send cards (phone/service.py:13-15) and scoped, expiring, max-use grants (governance/action_gate.py:524) exist.
- (§36-37) Phase 5 lists 'structured browser lane' and 'per-app permissions' as future work; services/browser_session.py (Playwright dedicated profile) and services/desktop_grants.py (none/observe/act per exe) exist.
- (§36-37) Phase 2/6/7 list off-record guarantees, third-party deletion and workspace rollback as future work; services/off_record.py, services/forget_person.py and routes/workspace_undo.py:30-45 already exist (partial in scope).
- (§36-37) 37.6 frames build-time handler-enumeration tests as migration work; tests/unit/test_every_action_is_governed.py already enumerates registered handlers and fails on direct invocation paths (:203, :213).
- (§36-37) 37.2 lists connector health as a divergent system; it is already consolidated in services/connector_health.py (summarise :364) with connector_protocol/connector_registry.
- (§36-37) Phase 8 treats the marketplace as a future optional step after governance is proven; a marketplace already ships with buying enabled by default (services/marketplace.py:415).
- (§36-37) The input row NS-36.0-10 claimed there is no rollback anywhere (grep rollback: none). That is wrong: services/boot_guard.py implements a health-checked auto-rollback of self-edits, wired at server.py:1151-1193.
- (§36-37) The input row NS-36.8-6 claimed there is no revocation mechanism. That is wrong: extension_security.py:504 remove_from_allowlist revokes MCP server approvals.

| NS-6.1-3 | MUST | The owner controls which actions need approval and which recurring grants exist. | **shipped** | action_gate grants plus UI revoke (index.html:45679-45715); approval cards |  |  |
| NS-6.1-4 | MUST | The owner controls what Friday remembers and forgets. | **shipped** | services/forget_person.py (tombstone plus purge); off_record.py (tests/unit/test_off_record_writes_nothing.py); memory proposals approve/reject |  |  |
| NS-6.1-5 | MUST | The owner controls communication style, remote channels, export, and whether the installation exists. | **partial** | routes/persona.py; cli.py:1602 cmd_export, cmd_erase; packaging/windows/uninstall.ps1. Survey 4 reports /api/channels has no UI consumer, so remote-channel activation control is weak (not re-checked). |  |  |
| NS-6.1-6 | MUST NOT | Friday may disagree, warn or refuse against the safety floor, but must not manipulate the owner into granting broader authority. | **partial** | dissent_gate.py covers disagree/refuse. | NS-6.2-1 | MUST | The local control plane stays functional when all cloud providers are unavailable. | **partial** | Local seat bonsai2:27b; local_only_guard.py; tests/unit/test_offline_voice_resilience.py, test_local_only_seats_refuse_cloud.py. But the default routing mode is cloud_only (core/__init__.py:2582), and there is no whole-system offline test. |  |  |
| NS-6.3-1 | MUST | Every tool execution and outward action passes one canonical execution path. | **partial** | agent._execute_tool is the only handler caller; authorize_external covers compute and gmail (decision doc bypasses #3 and #19). The workspace_studio.py:468 patch path is the exception. |  |  |
| NS-6.3-2 | MUST | Static analysis and runtime tests fail if a handler can be invoked outside that path. | **shipped** | tests/unit/test_every_action_is_governed.py: runtime spy on every registered tool, plus an AST scan of src/ with a planted-call self-test and a REVIEWED_DIRECT_MCP allowlist |  |  |
| NS-6.4-1 | MUST NOT | Never silently switch local execution to cloud (or back). | **shipped** | local_only_guard.py refuses at the cloud transports; model_router._mode_filtered_attempts in agent.py:401; vault requests stay local (agent.py:444) |  | A6 |
| NS-6.4-2 | MUST NOT | Never silently switch one cloud provider to another. | **partial** | agent.py:413-440: the ladder falls back across providers within the mode automatically. It is journaled (ladder_fallback) and badged (attribution.note_fallback), but runs without prior authorization. |  | A6 |
| NS-6.4-3 | MUST NOT | Never silently switch one principal's context to another's. | **proposed** | There is no multi-principal model (core/__init__.py:3520-3530). Specced in v6-wholeness-spec.md P9 (person-boundary gate). |  |  |
| NS-6.4-4 | MUST NOT | Never silently turn a read into a write, a draft into a send, or an internal file into a shared artifact. | **shipped** | action_gate classify: drafting internal, send and publish outward (decision doc #5, #10, #15); gmail_send needs an approval card |  |  |
| NS-6.4-5 | MUST NOT | Never silently turn an unverified outcome into a completed state. | **partial** | The completed_unverified status exists (agent.py:3878). Known defects: the sentinel gap in completion_receipts.py:28-37 and the evidence gate counting any tool call. | goals-and-delivery-receipts.md P0 |  |
| NS-6.4-6 | MUST | Fallbacks are policy-authorized, visible and receipted. | **partial** | Fallbacks are journaled (task_journal ladder_fallback) and attributed. Voice egress_notice and egress_receipt go to the client (routes/voice.py:3875,4045). There is no signed fallback receipt and no policy authorization. |  |  |
| NS-6.5-1 | MUST | Never claim work complete without a delivery receipt; otherwise say unverified, waiting, partial or failed. | **partial** | completion_receipts.py claim law and completed_unverified. Defects: an approval card or hold counts as ok. Delivery receipts are not built. | docs/design/active/goals-and-delivery-receipts.md §5 |  |
| NS-6.6-1 | MUST NOT | Untrusted content cannot expand permissions, create grants, change governance, add recipients, or authorize outward actions. | **shipped** | services/taint.py; action_gate sends content-derived actions to a card a chat yes cannot satisfy; grants are owner-session only; the run_command local-API refusal (decision doc #1); tests/unit/test_taint_provenance.py |  |  |
| NS-6.7-1 | MUST NOT | Personality may shape communication but must not override honesty, privacy, approval, source integrity, isolation or the safety floor. | **shipped** | Governance is enforced in code independently of the prompt (action_gate.py); action_policy is appended last to every prompt and overrides are stripped (services/action_policy.py) |  |  |
| NS-6.8-1 | MUST | Visualizations of memory, reasoning, growth, goals or activity correspond to real system events. | **partial** | Reasoning traces are live from real data (index.html:36124 /api/traces/live); the activity page reads /api/activity (index.html:53423). | NS-6.8-2 | MUST | Decorative animation is clearly decorative; an orb, graph or avatar is never implied as evidence unless derived from trace data. | **partial** | MOODS table (index.html:4053) includes emotional states (EXCITED, CURIOUS, PROTECTIVE) with no 'decorative' labelling. Evolution names ('TRANSCENDENCE') imply growth. | docs/design/active/avatar-visual-genome.md | A1 |
| NS-6.9-1 | MUST | Express uncertainty when evidence is insufficient. | **partial** | SELF.md:100 prefers 'I don't know' to hallucinating; response_provenance.py marks unverified citations; live_state.py |  |  |
| NS-6.9-2 | MUST | Be able to refuse to infer sensitive characteristics, diagnose a person, or invent live state. | **partial** | live_state.py covers inventing live state. | NS-6.10-1 | MUST | A complete export and uninstall path is a core product requirement. | **partial** | cli.py:1602 cmd_export (plus the Settings export button per data_export.py); packaging/windows/uninstall.ps1; cmd_erase. There is no import or restore: v6 P8 /api/self/import is not built. | v6-wholeness-spec.md Phase 8 |  |
| NS-6.10-2 | MUST | The owner can recover human-readable data, machine-readable data and a portable encrypted bundle without FutureSpeak infrastructure. | **partial** | The export zip of ~/.friday is machine-readable raw files. | NS-6.11-1 | MUST NOT | Never claim exclusive emotional need, discourage human relationships, or guilt the owner for leaving, resetting or disabling Friday. | **missing** | No such rule in governance/proof_of_integrity.py CLAWS_TEXT, SELF.md, action_policy.py or voice_persona.py (grep for guilt/dependency/relationship found nothing). No active spec covers it. |  |  |
| NS-6.11-2 | MUST NOT | Never imply sentience to persuade or fabricate distress when stopped. | **missing** | No guardrail or test found (grep for 'sentien' across src/agent_friday returned nothing) |  |  |
| NS-6.11-3 | MUST NOT | Never manipulate attachment to increase engagement. | **missing** | emotional_arc.py adapts tone from user sentiment, and no anti-engagement constraint is stated. No guard found. |  |  |
| NS-6.11-4 | MUST NOT | Never conceal that the personality is implemented through software and models. | **partial** | SELF.md describes Friday as a 'sovereign personal AI'; the model badge names the generating model (services/attribution.py). There is no explicit rule. |  |  |
| NS-6.12-1 | SHOULD | Feel alive through attentiveness, coherence and usefulness rather than spectacle. | **partial** | Dazzle off/subtle/full setting (index.html:1319-1350). Default is full per survey 7. |  | A1 |
| NS-6.12-2 | MUST NOT | Never require users to navigate spectacle to reach ordinary functions (a flat path exists). | **partial** | Flat windows and dock (index.html:5877 DOCK_GROUPS). Reduced motion is honored in CSS (index.html:1349, 1733), but the 3D scene loop does not check prefers-reduced-motion (survey 6; index.html:5111 time-lapse). |  | A1 |
| NS-7.2-1 | MUST NOT | The core product is not a creator marketplace. | **conflicting** | A Marketplace workspace is in the core dock (index.html:5957); services/marketplace.py; routes/federation.py:365 /api/marketplace/purchase. — workspace-ecosystem.md decided to disable purchases; v6 §1.1 stages the creator economy as future |  |  |
| NS-7.2-2 | MUST NOT | The core product is not a cryptocurrency or compute-rental economy. | **conflicting** | Positron (mψ) economy in services/economy.py with UI (index.html:16164, 16267); services/compute_provider.py accepts federated compute jobs (routes/compute.py:41 /api/federation/compute/request). |  |  |
| NS-7.2-3 | MUST NOT | Never publish, purchase, transfer funds, or delete irreplaceable data without explicit authority. | **partial** | action_gate treats publish and schedule-post as outward (decision doc #5). But marketplace DEFAULT_POLICY (services/marketplace.py:43-54) has buying enabled, require_approval only over 1,000,000 mψ, and selling auto_accept_at_listing_price True. |  |  |
| NS-7.2-4 | MUST NOT | Never run arbitrary third-party code without isolation. | **partial** | Peer compute jobs now need an owner card via authorize_external (decision doc #3), then run locally (python_script_adapter); services/code_sandbox.py exists. Survey 2 notes a wrong sandbox-confinement docstring. |  |  |
| NS-7.3-1 | MUST | Federation, agent-to-agent collaboration, skill marketplace and distributed compute stay absent from the core UX until the local product is dependable and security-reviewed. | **conflicting** | Marketplace is in the dock (index.html:5957); federation routes (routes/federation.py, routes/compute.py); peer and federation endpoints exposed. v6-wholeness-spec.md §1.1 'scope by subtraction' agrees they should be staged, but the code ships them. |  |  |

| Req | Strength | Requirement | Status | Evidence | Specs | Amend. |

| NS-11.1-1 | MUST | Architecture preserves identity, memory, governance, permissions and audit independently of any model provider. | **partial** | services/egress_gate.py (provider-agnostic seal_outbound); routing/model_router.py + services/model_router.py multi-provider ladders; governance/action_gate.py; services/activity_ledger.py — Governance/memory/audit are local and provider-independent in practice, but no formal plane separation. |  |  |
| NS-11.1-2 | MUST | Seven planes may share one process, but their interfaces and trust boundaries must be explicit. | **partial** | Single Flask process (server.py app.run :1230); explicit trust boundaries exist only for egress (services/egress_gate.py docstring 'Router vs EgressGate') and phone ingress (phone/ingress.py separate Flask app) — Most other planes are cross-importing modules; survey 3 notes duplicated core tool lists. |  |  |
| NS-11.3-1 | SHOULD | Canonical install runs a loopback-only local service controlled by a desktop shell. | **shipped** | server.py:995-998 default bind 127.0.0.1; friday_tray.py start_server/stop_server/restart_server (:324-395) |  |  |
| NS-11.3-2 | MUST | Bind only to approved local interfaces by default. | **shipped** | server.py:997 FRIDAY_BIND_HOST default 127.0.0.1; :1000-1022 refuses non-loopback bind without auth key |  |  |
| NS-11.3-3 | MUST | Require authentication for proxied, tunneled or remote requests. | **shipped** | core/__init__.py:559 _looks_proxied, :578 _is_local_request, :3506 check_auth fail-closed 403 without FRIDAY_REMOTE_KEY |  |  |
| NS-11.3-4 | MUST | Separate phone/remote ingress from privileged local routes. | **shipped** | phone/ingress.py:1-40 separate Flask app 'friday_phone_ingress' on its own 127.0.0.1 port; writes validated events to spool, does not act; services/observer_access.py read-only remote credential |  |  |
| NS-11.3-5 | MUST | Expose a health endpoint containing no sensitive content. | **partial** | routes/core_routes.py:385 /api/health returns uptime, provider health, version, but also mood, memory/vault entry counts (:437-455) — Counts/mood are low sensitivity but not content-free; behind app-wide auth hook. |  |  |
| NS-11.3-6 | MUST | Maintain one authoritative process registry. | **partial** | services/residency_arbiter.py owns llama-server processes; services/arbiter.py leases table; survey 3: two boot reconcilers (agent.py:3295 vs server.py:474) — No single registry across all subprocesses (voice workers, ComfyUI, MCP servers). |  |  |
| NS-11.3-7 | MUST | Use a single-instance lock. | **shipped** | server.py:793 _acquire_single_instance_lock (friday_server.lock); friday_tray.py:597 _claim_windows_mutex; tests/unit/test_single_instance_lock.py |  |  |
| NS-11.3-8 | MUST | Shut down cleanly. | **partial** | friday_tray.py:690-695 SIGINT/SIGTERM handler, stop_server :361; server.py has no atexit/signal shutdown hook (grep) — Clean shutdown is tray-driven; server relies on kill. |  |  |
| NS-11.3-9 | MUST | Recover incomplete tasks and migrations on restart. | **partial** | services/task_resume.py checkpoint resume; task_journal.reconcile_on_boot; services/boot_guard.py auto-revert to known-good state — Task resume and boot rollback exist; no general migration-recovery framework. |  |  |

| NS-11.4-1 | MUST | Desktop shell owns lifecycle, secure startup, tray, push-to-talk/transcribe shortcut, native notifications. | **partial** | friday_tray.py: start/stop/restart server, tray menu :443, _start_push_to_transcribe :543, _notify :583; services/push_to_talk.py — Descriptive list ('owns'); treated as requirement. |  |  |
| NS-11.4-2 | MUST | Desktop shell owns deep links, local file pickers, safe-mode launch, update/rollback, OS auth prompts. | **partial** | services/boot_guard.py FRIDAY_SAFE_MODE + auto-revert; routes/updates.py notify-only update check (no in-app update/rollback); no OS protocol handler, native file picker, or OS auth prompt found in packaging/ or src — Safe mode env var and boot rollback exist; others missing. |  |  |

| NS-11.7-3 | MUST | Every store declares schema version, encryption policy, owner scope, migration, backup, retention, corruption detection, recovery. | **partial** | No per-store declaration exists, and no PRAGMA integrity_check/user_version is used anywhere in src/agent_friday (grep). What does exist: a single global classification of ~/.friday files for export and backup in services/data_export.py:1-50 (TRANSIENT_PARTS, DOWNLOAD_TOPS, SECRET_DIRS, skip_reason) and a passphrase-encrypted full backup (`friday export --full`). A few artifacts are versioned on their own: routing/provider_descriptors.py:80 SCHEMA_VERSION=2 with a migration at :481/:574, and health_check.py:99. — Backup and export are covered globally. The per-store manifest the spec asks for, with retention, corruption detection and recovery for each store, is missing. [corrected by verifier] |  |  |
| NS-11.8-1 | SHOULD | Use an internal event bus for state changes. | **partial** | There is no general-purpose bus. Other state changes (tasks, seats, settings) do not go through a shared bus. [corrected by verifier] |  |  |

| NS-27.1-1 | MUST | Owner MUST be able to carry Friday's durable self to another supported device. | **partial** | src/agent_friday/cli.py:1602 cmd_export (--full encrypted .fbak), cli.py cmd_decrypt_backup; routes/insights.py:405 /api/data/export — Export exists; no import/restore path (only decrypt-backup back to a zip, manual unpack). | docs/design/active/v6-wholeness-spec.md Phase 8 (whole-self export & restore) |  |
